Run both restoration commands immediately if a production verification check fails.

```powershell
vercel rollback dpl_7M6Jy4gZetTzM28ppd4AmbcS2s3a --scope flash2404s-projects --yes
railway api 'mutation { deploymentRollback(id: "734e4a9f-4a18-452b-81e3-4d74e8da3f24") }'
```

The Railway mutation was verified against the current authenticated API schema. It identifies the recorded production deployment explicitly. The Vercel command restores the production aliases to the recorded deployment. Confirm both deployment identities and health after restoring them. The old alias uses its original isolated backend rewrite, recorded separately from the direct production API in `release-20261105/alias-health-before.json` and `release-20261105/railway-health-before.json`.

Restore any release environment variables from the private pre-release snapshot after rollback. Preserve the production volume. Retain the pre-release volume backup for state recovery if needed. Recheck automatic Vercel alias assignment against the recorded value after the release or rollback.

The Git history remains available for an ordinary follow-up revert commit if needed. A merge revert uses `git revert -m 1 <merge-sha>`.
 ## Authorized production-volume backup  The state-write condition in `release-20261105/STATE_AUDIT.md` applied. Backup bytes remain exclusively at `/app/state/.release-backups/20261105-before-integrated` on the production volume. Only metadata was returned. All SQLite backups passed integrity checks, and source data-version checks detected no concurrent commit after their snapshots. The backup contains 26 SQLite databases and 1,163 files, including its manifest and restore program.  The temporary key `portwatch-release-20261105-temporary` (`SHA256:e25uo6Ewv3pEFYdPUsgcTsrwD526teSLztrQHxHYlBg`) was deleted in the same guarded registration/use/cleanup operation. A fresh Railway key query confirmed its absence. The local private and public key files were removed. Verified metadata is in `release-20261105/backup-file-metadata.json`.  For state restoration, run this exact command as the production service maintenance start command against the recorded rollback image, before any application worker starts. It verifies the backup hashes, retains the current files under the backup folder, restores the snapshot, then starts the API. Clear the maintenance override after recovery.  ```sh python /app/state/.release-backups/20261105-before-integrated/restore-state.py && exec uvicorn backend.app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port
